    def deco(*a: Any, **kw: Any) -> Any:
        from py4j.protocol import Py4JJavaError
    
        try:
            return f(*a, **kw)
        except Py4JJavaError as e:
            converted = convert_exception(e.java_exception)
            if not isinstance(converted, UnknownException):
